- **restore-pg-with-owner-fix**: USE WHEN restoring a service's gzipped postgres dump into a freshly-empty database during a database recovery or cross-host pg migration — `psql &lt;db&gt; &lt; dump` run as the postgres superuser leaves every table owned by `postgres`, breaks the service that reads its own `schema_version` under its service role (miniflux, immich, …), and restart-loops with `relation "schema_version" already exists (42P07)`. This skill runs drop + recreate + restore + the `ALTER OWNER` sweep across all tables + sequences + the public schema in one shot, so the trap doesn't bite a second time. ([phibkro/homelab](https://github.com/phibkro/homelab/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/restore-pg-with-owner-fix))
